2026-10-29T09:25:13.6104800Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Build and serve the PP4 bundle with MegaMoE 4-stage/tail-split, FA3 varlen N192, Prefill MoE input fusion and Decode SwapAB/norm selections

One build and one serving run for the 10k campaign, following the decoupled
PP4 result (run 40825417205: 8952.69, period 124 ms, Prefill at the 690 W cap,
PP latency and the bootstrap consensus keeping Decode at 160 requests).

Contents (first-parent merges on perf/pp4-10k-20261029):
- PP4 scheduler: stage 0 decides admission and later stages follow its rid
  list (bootstrap consensus removed), gloo send works are waited after one
  loop instead of accumulating, the last stage runs its result step before
  launching, async depth 1, concurrency 225, proxy hidden transfer over the
  copy engine (CUDA IPC slots and events, voted against NCCL on the first 16
  launches, NCCL fallback). Model prediction 9,338 at a 121.8 ms period.
- Prefill MegaMoE: a fourth pipeline stage via FC2 staging through the
  per-warp epilogue scratch, and <=64-row tail tiles split across both
  warpgroups (host patches, hence the rebuild). In-job selection on all 12
  Prefill GPUs, bit-identity or FP-order tolerance, fallback to 3 stages.
- Prefill FA3: register-direct FP8 output and a varlen tile scheduler so
  two-request microbatches use the fixed N192 path (fa3-verify rebuild),
  screened in job against the prepared N224 path.
- Prefill MoE input: post-attention add-RMSNorm writes the MegaMoE FP8 input
  and scales directly (pre_dispatch removed), optionally with a fused gate
  GEMM + top-k; selected in job on the Decode GPUs before model load.
- Decode: SwapAB QKV/O GEMMs for 129-176 rows, a 512-lane input norm/quant
  and a broadcast verify penalty, selected in job against the pinned Decode
  kernels.

Expected (all CPU-only so far): Prefill period about 117-119 ms, Decode step
about -5%, score about 9,500-9,600; 10k needs about 112 ms. No profile pass:
with the selections the predicted end is 12:25-13:25 and a profile pass would
leave less than 60 s of the 17-minute limit.

Judge by score.json, the selection logs (prefill MegaMoE, FA3 screen, MoE
input, Decode dense), "PP4 proxy transport: copy|nccl" in the Prefill logs,
the Prefill PP0 batch rate, #bootstrap-req near 0, Decode #running-req and
ITL p99.
